# OrderService

> Generated by `tools/build-service-docs.py`. **Do not edit.**

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Tier | commerce: The sale path. Highest availability, highest write rate. |
| Contracts | `orders`, `shift`, `payments` |
| Schemas owned | `orders`, `payments` |
| Operations in the slice | 78 of 249 |
| Scale | Write-heavy, spiky, latency-critical. The one that autoscales. |
| If it is down | Down means no sales. Highest availability target in the platform. |

## Why this is a service

**The transactional core.** 96 operations, 34 tables, and the highest write rate in the platform. Separate because its scaling profile is unlike anything else — a Saturday evening is ten times a Tuesday morning.

**`shift` belongs here and not in Tenancy.** A shift is venue-scoped, which is what made it look like a tenancy concern — but **venue scope is the platform's default rather than a service boundary**: 675 of 1,014 operations are venue-scoped.

**What decides the service is who owns the tables.** A shift holds cash, `orders.deposit_box` reconciles against payments, and `orders.cash_movement` posts to `ledger.posting` at close. **A till's takings and a till's drawer are the same money counted twice** — separating them makes every settlement a distributed join.

**Guest recurring billing lives here, decided by [ADR-0048](../../docs/adr/0048-guest-recurring-billing-is-commerce.md)** rather than in the Control Plane that provisions cells. `payments.dunning_policy` and `payments.dunning_case` are the two newest tables in the service. **A charge on a schedule fails differently from a charge at a till** — nobody is standing at the counter when a renewal declines, so the recovery path is a queue and a timetable rather than a second card.

## Group: cart

### abandonCart

**`DELETE /carts/{cartId}`**: Empty it deliberately

**Releases every lease immediately** rather than waiting for expiry. A guest who has decided not to buy should not hold capacity for another twenty minutes.
Distinct from expiry: this is a decision, and the recovery campaign must not chase someone who said no (22.3.6).

### addCartLine

**`POST /carts/{cartId}/lines`**: Add something

2.9.4. **Acquiring the lease is the whole reason this is a server operation.** A capacity-bound product added to a cart reserves capacity for a configured window, and a cart held in a browser cannot reserve anything.
**Refused rather than added where capacity has gone.** A line added optimistically and removed at checkout is a guest who thinks they bought something.
Returns the cart with any conflicts the addition created (2.9.5) — golf at 13:00 already in the cart and karting at 13:00 being added is a prompt, not a refusal, because a party of four may legitimately split.

### checkoutCart

**`POST /carts/{cartId}/checkout`**: Turn the cart into an order

**Transfers the leases to the order rather than releasing and re-acquiring them.** Releasing first would let another guest take the capacity between the two calls, which is the failure that makes a checkout feel unreliable at exactly the wrong moment.
The cart is retained, not deleted — a checkout that fails at payment must be resumable, and a guest whose card was declined should still have their seats.
**One cart, one order, one receipt** across ticketing, F&B and retail (14 August).
**No order against an unproven contact on the guest web or app** (decided 17 September 2026; the rule is on `identity` `verifyGuestEmail`). A signed-in session must be verified; where the site's `guestCheckout` feature is on, a guest without an account must have proved the email or mobile the tickets go to with a one-time code. **Kiosk walk-up orders are exempt** — the tickets are printed for the person standing there.
**This operation is the gate, and the cart is not** ([ADR-0045](../../docs/adr/0045-every-order-carries-a-proven-contact.md), 18 September 2026, matrix 2.6.1 §2.4). Anyone may fill a cart. The fork between signing in and supplying guest details belongs to the checkout page — `WEB-011`, `GST-041` — and this call refuses until one of them has completed, keeping the cart so the guest resumes rather than restarts.

### claimCart

**`POST /carts/{cartId}/claim`**: Attach an anonymous cart to a guest

2.1.31. **The operation that makes cross-channel work.** A guest who built a cart anonymously and then signs in keeps it; a guest who signs in on a laptop finds the cart they started on their phone.
Where the guest already has a cart, the two **merge rather than one replacing the other** — losing a cart at the moment of signing in is the worst possible time to lose one. Lines that cannot be re-leased are reported rather than dropped silently.

| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.cart`, `orders.cart_line`, `pii.subject` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.cart`, `orders.cart_line` |
| Called by | GST-041, WEB-016 |

### createCart

**`POST /carts`**: Start a cart

**A cart holds leases; an order holds money.** Different lifetimes, different failure modes, and merging them is why this contract did not exist until 17 August — `createOrder` takes a whole basket at once, which is correct for a till and cannot be resumed on another device.
Created against a guest subject where one is known, or an anonymous token where it is not. **The token is what lets a guest browse before signing in** and keep their cart when they do (2.1.31).
**POS does not use this.** A till builds its sale locally and submits atomically, because it works offline and does not need a resumable cart. Same order contract, different channel.

### extendCart

**`POST /carts/{cartId}/extend`**: Give the guest more time

Extends every lease in the cart. **Capped, and the cap is configuration** — an indefinitely extendable hold is capacity permanently removed from sale by someone who left the tab open.
Offered once, typically, and the interface should say it is the last extension rather than letting a guest discover the cap by hitting it.

### getCart

**`GET /carts/{cartId}`**: The cart, priced and checked, right now

2.9.2. **Re-prices and re-checks on every read**, because a cart is a claim about the future and the future moves. Availability, promotions and lease expiry are all live.
Returns the conflicts (2.9.5) and the leases with their remaining time, so the interface can say *"your seats are held for 6 minutes"* rather than discovering it at checkout.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | primary |
| Reads | `catalogue.channel_capacity`, `catalogue.inventory_hold`, `orders.cart`, `orders.cart_line`, `promotions.promotion` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | GST-009, GST-032, GST-041, GST-053, KSK-006, POS-010, PTR-010, WEB-010 |

### updateCartLine

**`PATCH /carts/{cartId}/lines/{lineId}`**: Change a quantity

Increasing extends the lease and may fail on capacity; decreasing releases part of it. **Releasing immediately matters** — a guest reducing from six to two should return four seats to sale now, not in twenty minutes.

|  |  |

| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |

## Group: cash

### createCashMovement

**`POST /shifts/{shiftId}/cash-movements`**: Record a cash lift or add

A lift removes cash from an open float mid-shift without closing it — the supervisor takes the excess to the safe and the cashier keeps trading. Both directions are traced, both record the authorising principal, and both adjust the expected figure used at close.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `CASH_LIFT` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | append |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.cash_count_line`, `orders.cash_movement` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.cash_count_line`, `orders.cash_movement` |
| Called by | BO-039, BO-040, BO-041, BO-042, EMP-009, POS-001, POS-007, POS-013, POS-017, POS-018 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| shiftId | path | yes | string |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**: `CreateCashMovementRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string | yes | Client-generated ULID. (pattern ^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$) |
| kind | CashMovementKind: enum (openingFloat, lift, add) | yes |  |
| amount | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| amount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| amount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| amount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| denominations | array of CashCountLine |  | A count is a list of lines and the line is the row. (min items 1) |
| denominations[].id | string (uuid) |  |  |
| denominations[].shiftId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| denominations[].depositBoxId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| denominations[].denominationId | string (uuid) | yes | References platform.denomination — face value, kind and sort order live there. |
| denominations[].countedQuantity | integer | yes | How many of this note or coin were in the drawer. (min 0) |
| denominations[].countedValue | Money |  | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| denominations[].countedValue.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| denominations[].countedValue.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |

## Group: dunning

### listMyPaymentIssues

**`GET /guests/me/payment-issues`**: A guest's own failed recurring payments

**Design gap, 23 September.** The membership billing statement in the design shows a declined renewal and lets the guest retry it. The recovery cases existed (BL-100) and only staff could see them. A soft decline (`declineClass: soft`) is retried on the venue's schedule anyway; this lets the guest act first.

|  |  |

| Scope level | tenant |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 2 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Reads | `payments.dunning_case` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | GST-015, WEB-023 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| pageSize | query |  | integer |  |
| cursor | query |  | string | Opaque cursor from a previous page. |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Open cases, newest first |

### retryMyDunningPayment

**`POST /dunning-cases/{caseId}/retry`**: Retry a declined membership payment now

Charges the case's amount again, on a different saved card if one is given. **A hard decline is never retried on the same card**, because the bank has said that card will not pay; the guest must choose another. Success resolves the case and the membership continues without a gap.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | tenant |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 2 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `payments.dunning_case` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `payments.dunning_case` |
| Called by | GST-015, WEB-023 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |
| caseId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |

**Request body**: `RetryDunningPaymentRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| paymentMethodId | string (uuid) |  | A saved card other than the one that failed. (nullable) |

**Response**: `DunningCase`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| subjectId | string (uuid) |  |  |
| orderId | string |  | The order whose renewal failed. |
| amount | Money |  | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| amount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| amount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| amount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| state | DunningState: enum (scheduled, inProgress, exhausted, recovered, resolvedManually) | yes | BL-100. |
| declineClass | object |  | From the most recent attempt. |
| attemptsMade | integer | yes |  |
| nextAttemptAt | string (date-time) |  | Null where the case has ended or the decline is hard. (nullable) |
| firstFailedAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |
| resolvedAt | string (date-time) |  | (nullable) |
| resolution | enum (paidByOtherMeans, cardReplaced, writeOff, cancelledByGuest, None) |  | (nullable) |
| resolutionNote | string |  | What resolveDunningCase was told, which had nowhere to land until now. (max length 500; nullable) |
| resolvedByPrincipalId | string (uuid) |  | Who closed it. (read-only; nullable) |
| scopePath | string |  | The partition key (ADR-0005). |

**Responses**

|---|---|---|

| 409 |  | The case is already resolved, or the decline is hard and no other card was given. |
| 422 |  | The payment was declined again. |


## Group: order

### appendEntitlementToMedia

**`POST /media/{mediaCode}/entitlements`**: Add something to a ticket the guest already holds

A guest arrives, buys admission, and an hour later wants a locker. They scan the QR they are already carrying and the locker attaches to it. **No second QR.**
Named as important on 14 August: *"I don't need to give another QR code for the locker. I will use the same QR code and I can go and scan it in the locker."*
This creates a **new order** that issues entitlements onto **existing media**. The original order is untouched — it was paid, receipted and possibly reported on, and editing it to add a later purchase would make yesterday's revenue move.
The media is the join, not the order. That is why this operation is keyed on `mediaCode` rather than on an order id: the guest has the wristband in their hand and may not know which order issued it.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_CREATE` |
| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `access.entitlement`, `cache:idempotency`, `orders.order_line`, `orders.payment`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `access.entitlement`, `cache:idempotency`, `orders.order_line`, `orders.payment`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-027, CMS-010, EMP-036, POS-010 |

### applyManualDiscount

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/discounts`**: Apply a discount a cashier chose

Distinct from a promotion, which is a rule the platform evaluates. This is a person deciding — service recovery, a damaged item, a manager's judgement.
**Always requires a reason, and above the venue threshold an approver who is not the requester.** Manual discount is the most common internal fraud in retail, and the control is that every one is attributable and reviewable rather than that they are forbidden.
Posts to a discount account, never as a price change. A line sold at a reduced price and a line discounted are different facts, and margin reporting needs both.

### createOrder

**`POST /orders`**: Create an order

The client submits lines with the prices it quoted from its local bundle. **The server re-prices every line** and returns both figures.
Where they differ the order is still accepted at the quoted price and `priceVariance` is populated. The client shows nothing; the variance is a finance concern, not a cashier concern.
Offline-capable. The client writes to its local journal, acknowledges the cashier, and replays through `/sync/orders` on reconnect.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_CREATE` |
| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | append |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.order_line`, `orders.payment`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.order_line`, `orders.payment`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-022, BO-023, BO-026, BO-047, BO-051, BO-070, BO-130, EMP-014, EMP-034, GST-009, POS-002, POS-004, POS-005, POS-006, POS-013, PTR-002, PTR-005, PTR-008, PTR-015, PTR-016, WEB-012 |

### exchangeOrderLines

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/exchanges`**: Exchange lines for different products or dates

A date change, a tier upgrade, a different performance. **Settles only the difference** — modelled as separate refund and sale, a guest exchanging a date can end up refunded and then unable to rebook when the new date turns out to be full.
The replacement is held before the original is released, never the other way round.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_EXCHANGE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-022, BO-023, BO-026, BO-047, BO-051, BO-070, EMP-014, EMP-034, POS-002, POS-006, POS-010, POS-011, PTR-002, PTR-005, PTR-008, PTR-015, PTR-016 |

| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

| incomingLines[].eligibilityDeclaration[].ageYears | integer |  | (nullable) |

### getMediaEntitlements

### getOrderCalendarEvent

**`GET /orders/{orderId}/calendar-event`**: The visit as a calendar entry

**`GST-018 Add to Calendar / Reminders` had no calendar operation**, only the order and a wallet pass, so the screen named after a calendar could not put anything in one.
Returns an iCalendar (RFC 5545) event per dated line: the venue, the session start and end, and a link back to the booking. **The event carries no ticket and no QR code**, because a calendar is synced to places a ticket should not go. Rescheduling the order changes the event UID's sequence, so a calendar that re-imports it updates the entry rather than adding a second one.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 3 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `orders.sales_order`, `orders.order_line`, `platform.scope` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | GST-018 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| orderId | path | yes | string |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | One VEVENT per dated line of the order. |
| 404 | NotFound | The resource does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope |
| 409 |  | The order has no dated line, so there is nothing to put in a calendar. |

### holdOrder

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/hold`**: Park a sale and free the till

The guest has gone to fetch a wallet, or wants to add something after checking with someone. The cashier parks the sale and serves the next person.
**Not a reservation.** Nothing is held against inventory — a parked sale that reserved capacity would let a queue of parked sales exhaust a session. Prices are re-evaluated on resume, and a price that moved is surfaced rather than silently applied.
Held orders expire. A till that accumulates parked sales across a shift cannot be closed, and the cashier who parked them has gone home.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_MODIFY` |
| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | lastWriterWins |

| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.order_line`, `orders.payment`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-022, BO-023, BO-026, BO-047, BO-051, BO-070, EMP-014, EMP-034, POS-002, POS-006, PTR-002, PTR-005, PTR-008, PTR-015, PTR-016 |

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |

| orderId | path | yes | string |  |

**Request body**

| holdUntil | string (date-time) |  |  |
| recordedAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |

### modifyOrder

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/modify`**: Add or remove lines on an existing order

Adding a line charges the difference; removing one refunds it under the venue's refund policy. **A modification is not a void and re-sale** — the order number, the guest's booking reference and the entitlements already issued all survive.
Lines whose entitlement has been redeemed cannot be removed. The guest has used it.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_MODIFY` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.order_line`, `orders.payment`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.order_line`, `orders.payment`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-022, BO-023, BO-026, BO-047, BO-051, BO-070, EMP-014, EMP-034, POS-002, POS-006, PTR-002, PTR-005, PTR-008, PTR-015, PTR-016 |

### reprintOrder

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/reprints`**: Reprint or resend tickets

Reprint count is recorded per line. Repeated reprints on one order is a signal worth surfacing — usually a printer fault, occasionally something else.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_REPRINT` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | append |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-022, BO-023, BO-026, BO-047, BO-051, BO-070, EMP-014, EMP-034, GST-010, POS-002, POS-006, POS-026, PTR-002, PTR-005, PTR-008, PTR-015, PTR-016, WEB-013 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| orderId | path | yes | string |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| delivery | enum (print, email, sms, whatsapp, wallet) | yes |  |
| destination | string |  |  |
| lineIds | array of string |  |  |
| reason | enum (printerFault, guestRequest, lostTicket, notReceived, other) |  |  |

**Response**: `object`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |

**Responses**

| 200 |  | Reissued |
| 400 | BadRequest | Validation failed |

### rescheduleOrder

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/reschedule`**: Move an order to another performance

The common case of an exchange, and worth its own operation because it is what a call centre agent does forty times a day. Same product, different date or time.
Where the new performance is priced differently, the balance settles as for an exchange. Where the venue charges a rescheduling fee, it appears as a line.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_RESCHEDULE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-022, BO-023, BO-026, BO-047, BO-051, BO-070, EMP-014, EMP-034, POS-002, POS-006, PTR-002, PTR-005, PTR-008, PTR-015, PTR-016 |

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |

| orderId | path | yes | string |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|

|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Rescheduled |
| 409 |  | Target performance is unavailable or outside the reschedule window |

### resumeOrder

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/resume`**: Bring a parked sale back to a till

Any till, not only the one that parked it — the cashier who took it may be on a break.
Prices are re-evaluated. Where a price, a promotion or an availability has moved since the hold, the response says so and the cashier decides. Silently charging the old price loses money; silently charging the new one loses the guest.

| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |

### setVisitReminder

**`PUT /orders/{orderId}/reminder`**: Turn a visit reminder on or off

**The reminders half of `GST-018` had nothing behind it.** A guest can ask to be reminded a chosen time before each dated line of the booking, on the channels they pick.
**Only channels the guest has consented to are used.** A channel chosen here but withdrawn in their communication preferences is skipped rather than sent, because a reminder is not a reason to message somebody who said no. Sent through the notification catalogue; a rescheduled booking moves its reminder with it.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 3 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `orders.visit_reminder` |
| Writes | `orders.visit_reminder` |
| Called by | GST-018 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| orderId | path | yes | string |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**: `VisitReminder`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string (uuid) |  | (read-only) |
| orderId | string |  | (read-only) |
| subjectId | string (uuid) |  | The guest who set it. (read-only) |
| enabled | boolean | yes |  |
| leadTimeMinutes | integer |  | How long before each session starts. (min 15; max 10080; default 1440) |
| channels | array of enum (push, email, sms) |  | (default ['push']) |
| scopePath | string |  | The partition key (ADR-0005). (read-only) |

**Response**: `VisitReminder`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string (uuid) |  | (read-only) |
| orderId | string |  | (read-only) |
| subjectId | string (uuid) |  | The guest who set it. (read-only) |
| enabled | boolean | yes |  |
| leadTimeMinutes | integer |  | How long before each session starts. (min 15; max 10080; default 1440) |
| channels | array of enum (push, email, sms) |  | (default ['push']) |
| scopePath | string |  | The partition key (ADR-0005). (read-only) |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Saved |
| 400 | BadRequest | Validation failed |
| 404 | NotFound | The resource does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope |

### transferOrderTickets

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/transfer`**: Transfer tickets to another guest

Per-ticket, not whole-order — a family splitting tickets between two phones is the normal case.
The recipient receives a claim link. **Ownership moves only when they claim it**, so a transfer to a mistyped address expires rather than stranding the ticket somewhere nobody can reach.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |

| Writes | `access.entitlement`, `cache:idempotency`, `orders.ticket_transfer` |
| Called by | GST-008, GST-009, GST-010, GST-012, GST-013, GST-014, GST-019, GST-045, GST-055, KSK-009, KSK-010, KSK-012, WEB-012, WEB-013, WEB-018, WEB-019, WEB-023, WEB-030 |

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |

| orderId | path | yes | string |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**

| recipient | object | yes |  |

**Responses**

## Group: orders

### createResaleListing

**`POST /resale-listings`**: List an entitlement for resale

BL-060. **A resale is a transfer with money attached, and the venue stays in the middle** — the seller's entitlement is voided and a new one issued to the buyer, so **the ticket that admits is always one the venue issued.** That is what stops a screenshot at the gate.
Refuses where the entitlement is partly consumed, name-bound, or past its resale window.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_CREATE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |

### createTicketTemplate

**`POST /ticket-templates`**: Create a ticket template

**Selection is automatic.** `selectionPriority`, `mediaType`, `appliesToProductKinds` and `appliesToChannels` decide which template a sale issues from, so the caller never chooses one. Two active templates with the same priority for the same product kind, channel and media type are refused, because the choice between them would be an accident of row order.
Check the artwork with `printTicketProof` before activating.

| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.ticket_template` |
| Called by | BO-346 |

| isRecyclable | boolean |  |  |
| recycleAfterDays | integer |  | (min 0; nullable) |

### getBillingStatement

**`GET /billing-statements/{statementId}`**: One statement, with its lines

BL-100. **The lines are the point.** A total with no breakdown is what a guest rings a call centre about, and the call centre has no more than they do.

### getGroupBooking

**`GET /group-bookings/{groupBookingId}`**: A group, its leader and its manifest

BL-028. **`orders.group_booking` had ten columns and nothing read it** — a table built and never surfaced.
**`confirmedSize` against `minimumSize` is the commercial edge.** A group that books for forty and confirms twenty-two has either a shortfall to pay or a release to make, and which one depends on a term nobody can see without this.
**`attendeeCaptureRequired` is a legal act, not a preference** — a school group at a venue with a waiver requirement cannot admit an unnamed child.

### getPaymentLink

**`GET /payment-links/{token}`**: What a guest holding a link is being asked to pay for

BL-072. **The operation the link needed and did not have.** `createPaymentLink` was contracted and nothing could open one.
**The caller is anonymous.** No session, no account, and quite possibly no intention of creating one — a guest who booked by phone is the case where they have not registered. **The token is the credential**, scoped to one order.
Returns the lines, the total and the deadline. **Never the guest's other orders**, never their profile, and never enough to identify them beyond what they already know.

### listBillingStatements

**`GET /billing-statements`**: What was charged, when, and against which agreement

2.14.19-2.14.23, 5.7.96, BL-100. **A guest paying monthly could see their pass and not what they had been charged for it.** Auto-renewal, grace and mandates all existed; the record a guest reads was the piece missing.
**A statement is not a tax invoice.** `isTaxInvoice` is false and read-only. UAE e-invoicing is Peppol five-corner with the FTA as the fifth corner, PINT AE XML and 51 mandatory fields, and **CF-133 is open on it** — a statement that implied otherwise would be wrong in the one direction with a regulator at the end of it.
**A guest sees their own and nothing else** (`x-ticvai-self-scoped: subject`); supplying another subject is 403 rather than silently honoured.

| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | tenant |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 2 |
| Offline | no |
| Read routing | replica |
| Reads | `orders.payment`, `payments.dunning_case`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | GST-015, WEB-023 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| from | query |  | string (date) |  |
| to | query |  | string (date) |  |
| pageSize | query |  | integer |  |
| cursor | query |  | string | Opaque cursor from a previous page. |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Statements |

### payByLink

**`POST /payment-links/{token}/pay`**: Pay for a booking taken at a till

BL-072. **Payment against the link, by somebody with no account.**
**Converts on success, and that is a decision rather than a mechanism** (ADR-0027). The reservation becomes an order the moment the money lands, because there is no staff member present at 11pm to press a button — **and a paid reservation that is still a reservation is inventory the venue thinks it can sell.**
**The card never reaches the platform.** Tokenised at the gateway like every other payment (CF-131), and the link carries no card data at any point.

| Called by | GST-009, WEB-014 |

### reissueEntitlement

**`POST /entitlements/{entitlementId}/reissue`**: Zero-value reissue of an expired entitlement for a later date

BL-084. **A goodwill act with no money in it**, and treating it as a refund plus a sale puts two transactions through the ledger for a gesture that moved nothing.
Voids the old, issues the new. **The reason is recorded and the original is traceable**, because a reissue with no audit is indistinguishable from a ticket appearing from nowhere.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_EXCHANGE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |

### requestGroupBooking

**`POST /group-booking-requests`**: Ask for a school trip or a birthday party

**Design gap, 23 September.** The guest-facing half of `createGroupBooking`: a school or a parent asks online, the date is held at once, and the booking starts `provisional`.

- **School:** paid by invoice; a quote and a risk assessment are sent within two working days; the final headcount is due five days before (`finalHeadcountDueBy`); one group pass is issued.
- **Party:** a deposit is taken under the venue's `DepositPolicy`, the balance is due on the day, and it is refundable in full up to the policy's cut-off.

### setPaymentProvider

**`PUT /payment-providers`**: Configure a gateway and its routing

CF-131. **Network International and Stripe for Phase 1** — two gateways, which is the number that makes routing a real question rather than a future one.
**The credential goes to the vault and never to the database.** `credentialRef` is what is stored, following the rule ADR-0020 set for AI providers and applied here: **no surface ever holds a provider key.**

### shareEntitlement

**`POST /entitlements/{entitlementId}/share`**: Let somebody else use this, without giving it away

BL-014. **Transfer is contracted and sharing is not, and they are different acts.** A transfer changes the holder; a share lets a second person present the same entitlement while the first still owns it.
**The owner can revoke; the recipient cannot transfer on.** That asymmetry is the whole point — a shared family pass should not become a resale chain.
Governed by `EntitlementTemplate.canShareMedia`, which already exists and had nothing behind it.

| Called by | GST-072, WEB-018 |

### storePaymentToken

**`POST /payment-tokens`**: Save a payment method for future use

BL-116. **The keystone.** Recurring membership billing, wallet auto-reload, one-click checkout and payment links all wait on this, and none could be designed until it existed.
**The card never reaches the platform.** The provider tokenises and returns a reference, which is what keeps PCI scope where it belongs.
**Requires its own consent purpose.** Storing a card for future use is not the same act as paying now, and a token taken without it is a card kept on a guest's behalf that they never agreed to.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_CREATE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 2 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `marketing.consent_record`, `payments.provider`, `payments.token` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `marketing.consent_record`, `payments.token` |
| Called by | GST-071, WEB-021 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| providerId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| providerToken | string (password) | yes |  |
| consentPurposeId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| setDefault | boolean |  | (default False) |

**Response**: `PaymentToken`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| subjectId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| providerId | string (uuid) | yes |  |

| isDefault | boolean | yes |  |
| consentPurposeId | string (uuid) |  | Storing a card for future use is a purpose a guest consents to, separate from the payment they are making now. (nullable) |

**Responses**

|---|---|---|
| 201 |  | Stored |

### updateTicketTemplate

**`PATCH /ticket-templates/{templateId}`**: Change or retire a ticket template

**Tickets already issued keep the template they were issued from.** Changing artwork affects the next ticket, never one a guest already holds. Retire with `isActive: false` rather than deleting, so an issued ticket can still be explained.

## Group: payment

### addTip

**`POST /payments/{paymentId}/tip`**: Record a tip against a payment

A separate operation rather than a line on the order, because a tip is not revenue — it is money held for staff and posts to a liability, not to sales. Treating it as a line inflates takings and understates what is owed.
Where the terminal captured the tip, this records what it reported. Where the guest wrote it on a slip, a cashier enters it, and that path needs the same attribution as a manual discount.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_MODIFY` |
| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | append |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.payment` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.payment` |
| Called by | BO-024, EMP-035, POS-005, PTR-012 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| paymentId | path | yes | string |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| amount | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| amount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| amount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| amount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| source | enum (terminalPrompt, cashierEntered, guestApp, serviceCharge) | yes | serviceCharge is not a tip and is separated deliberately — it is revenue in most jurisdictions, and pooling it with tips is how a payroll dispute starts. |
| allocateToPrincipalId | string (uuid) |  | Where the venue allocates rather than pools. |
| recordedAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |

**Response**: `Payment`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string | yes |  |
| orderId | string | yes |  |
| tender | TenderKind: enum (cash, card, wallet, voucher, bankTransfer, hotelCharge, installment, giftCard, …) | yes |  |
| tenderCurrency | string |  | 4.6.11. (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| tenderAmount | object |  | The amount in tenderCurrency, at that currency's own scale. |
| tenderAmount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| tenderAmount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| tenderAmount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| fxRate | number |  | The rate applied, stored on the payment rather than looked up later (CF-37). (nullable) |
| fxRateSource | enum (manual, feed, cardScheme) |  | 4.2.8. (nullable) |
| changeCurrency | string |  | 4.6.11 is deliberately asymmetric: accept foreign currency, refund in local. (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$; nullable) |
| amount | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| amount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| amount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| amount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| changeAmount | Money |  | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| changeAmount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| changeAmount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| changeAmount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| status | enum (authorised, captured, pendingConfirmation, declined, failed, voided, refunded) | yes |  |
| providerName | string |  | (nullable) |
| providerReference | string |  | (nullable) |
| lastInquiryAt | string (date-time) |  | (nullable) |
| recordedAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |
| syncedAt | string (date-time) |  | (nullable) |

### createPayment

**`POST /payments`**: Take a payment against an order

Cash is offline-capable. Card, wallet and voucher are not — they need the gateway.
A card payment returns `pendingConfirmation` when the terminal has been instructed but no result has arrived. **Do not assume failure.** Poll `/payments/{id}/inquiry`.

### inquirePaymentStatus

**`POST /payments/{paymentId}/inquiry`**: Ask the provider what actually happened

The recovery path (12 Aug §12). A terminal charged the card and the response never arrived — the guest has been charged and the order shows unpaid.
This queries the provider directly and reconciles. A background reconciler runs the same operation for payments left unconfirmed past a threshold, so recovery does not depend on an operator noticing.

## Group: refund

### createRefund

**`POST /orders/{orderId}/refunds`**: Refund an order, wholly or in part

**Not offline-capable.** A refund touches the gateway and the ledger; both need the network.
The venue's refund policy determines whether a second authoriser is required. Call `/venues/{venueId}/refund-policy` first, or submit without `secondaryAuthorisation` and handle the 409 that names the threshold.
Sequencing is ledger-first: the ledger entry is written, then the gateway is called, then the outcome is reconciled. A gateway call that succeeds against an unwritten ledger entry is money that left with no record.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_REFUND` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.payment`, `orders.refund`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `ledger.journal_entry`, `ledger.posting`, `orders.refund`, `platform.outbox` |
| Called by | ADM-616, BO-022, BO-023, BO-026, BO-047, BO-1147, EMP-014, EMP-034, POS-002, POS-006, POS-011, PTR-008, PTR-015 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| orderId | path | yes | string |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**: `CreateRefundRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string | yes | (pattern ^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$) |
| lineIds | array of string |  | Omit to refund the whole order. |
| amount | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| amount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| amount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| amount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| reason | string | yes | (min length 3; max length 500) |
| secondaryAuthorisation | object |  | Required above the venue's requiresSecondUserAbove. |
| secondaryAuthorisation.principalId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| secondaryAuthorisation.credential | string | yes | (max length 512) |
| refundToOriginalTender | boolean |  | (default True) |
| alternateTender | TenderKind: enum (cash, card, wallet, voucher, bankTransfer, hotelCharge, installment, giftCard, …) |  |  |
| recordedAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |

**Response**: `Refund`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string | yes |  |
| orderId | string | yes |  |
| fxRate | number |  | The rate on the original payment, not today's (BL-087, CF-118). (read-only; nullable) |
| taxReversalEntryId | string (uuid) |  | A refund reverses the tax entry it created, and this is where that is stated rather than implied. (read-only; nullable) |
| settleTo | enum (originalTender, advanceBalance, wireTransfer, storeCredit) |  | BL-086. (default originalTender) |
| fxVariance | object |  | Where the sale rate and the current rate differ, the difference is booked as an FX variance rather than hidden in the refund. |
| fxVariance.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| fxVariance.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| fxVariance.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| amount | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| amount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| amount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| amount.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| appliedPercentage | number |  | From the venue's time bands, or an approver override. |
| status | enum (pendingApproval, pendingGateway, completed, declined, failed) | yes |  |
| reason | string |  |  |
| requestedByPrincipalId | string (uuid) |  |  |
| secondaryPrincipalId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| approvedByPrincipalId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| ledgerEntryId | string |  | Written before the gateway is called. (nullable) |
| gatewayReference | string |  | (nullable) |
| createdAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |
| completedAt | string (date-time) |  | (nullable) |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 202 |  | Accepted. |
| 400 | BadRequest | Validation failed |
| 403 | Forbidden | Authenticated but not permitted at the requested scope |
| 409 |  | Second authorisation required and absent, refund window closed, or the amount exceeds what remains refundable. |

### createRefundRequest

**`POST /refund-requests`**: Guest-initiated refund request

Raised from the guest app. Enters the operations approval queue rather than refunding directly — a guest cannot self-serve money out of the ledger.
**The order must belong to the authenticated subject.** A request against another guest's order is rejected with 403, not queued for someone to notice later.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.refund_policy`, `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.refund` |
| Called by | ADM-610, BO-028, GST-067, WEB-019 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| orderId | string | yes |  |
| lineIds | array of string |  |  |
| reason | string | yes | (min length 3; max length 1000) |

**Response**: `object`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| requestId | string |  |  |
| status | enum (submitted, moreInfoRequested, approved, declined) |  |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 403 |  | Order does not belong to the authenticated subject |
| 202 |  | Submitted to the approval queue |

### approveShiftOpen

**`POST /shifts/{shiftId}/approve-open`**: Approve a shift opening outside tolerance

A float that does not match the venue's expected amount holds the shift in `pendingApproval` until a supervisor accepts it. Without this the cashier cannot trade, and with it the variance at close has a known starting point.

### closeDepositBoxes

**`POST /deposit-boxes/close`**: Close one box or all of them

5.8.6. **Automated close-out of one or more boxes.** A venue closing at 23:00 with forty tills does not close them one at a time, and a box whose holder has gone home still has to be reconciled.
A box closed without its holder present is flagged rather than blocked — **the cash is counted by somebody, and who counted it is the record that matters.**

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `SHIFT_CLOSE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `ledger.fx_rate`, `orders.cash_count_line`, `orders.deposit_box` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `ledger.posting`, `orders.deposit_box` |
| Called by | BO-024, POS-007 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| boxIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| allOpenAtVenue | boolean |  |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Closed, with a variance per box |

### closeShift

**`POST /shifts/{shiftId}/close`**: Blind close-out

The operator submits a counted denomination breakdown. The expected figure is not returned before submission — that is what makes the count blind.
The response carries the variance. Where it exceeds the configured threshold, the shift moves to `pendingVariance` and requires OVERSHORT_ACCEPT to finalise.
Closing a shift opened by another principal requires SHIFT_CLOSE_OTHER.

| Permission | `SHIFT_CLOSE` |

| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |

### listDenominations

**`GET /denominations`**: The notes and coins a till counts

Raised by Tanmay in the 20 August review, and **the table was built and nothing read it** — found by auditing which tables no operation names.
**Ordered for counting, not by value.** A cashier counts notes highest first and coins in the order they sit in the tray, and `sortOrder` is what makes the count screen match the physical drawer.
**Inactive denominations are returned by default and marked**, because a till counting a note withdrawn last year still has to record the one in the drawer.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `SHIFT_OPEN` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Reads | `platform.denomination`, `platform.region_settings` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | BO-1065, POS-001, POS-007, POS-011, POS-012, POS-013 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| includeInactive | query |  | boolean |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Denominations, in counting order |

### listDepositBoxes

**`GET /deposit-boxes`**: Cash boxes and who holds them

5.8.1. **A deposit box is allocated to a cashier, not to a till.** A cashier moving between workstations takes their float with them, and a workstation used by three people in a day has three boxes through it.
That separation is what makes a variance attributable to a person rather than to a counter.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `SHIFT_OPEN` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | primary |
| Reads | `identity.principal`, `orders.deposit_box` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | POS-015 |

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| openOnly | query |  | boolean |  |
| pageSize | query |  | integer |  |
| cursor | query |  | string | Opaque cursor from a previous page. |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Boxes |

### openShift

### recordNoSale

**`POST /shifts/{shiftId}/no-sale`**: Open the drawer without a sale

Change for a guest, a dropped coin, correcting a float. Legitimate and routine.
**Recorded because it is the classic cover for theft.** A drawer that opens forty times in a shift with four sales is a conversation, and it is only a conversation if someone can see the count. A reason is required for the same reason it is required on a manual discount — attributable, not forbidden.

|  |  |

| Permission | `SHIFT_SUSPEND` |
| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | append |

| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.no_sale_event` |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string | yes | (pattern ^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$) |
| reason | enum (changeForGuest, correctFloat, retrieveDroppedCash, tillCheck, other) | yes |  |
| note | string |  | (max length 300) |

| 409 |  | Shift is not open |

### reopenShift

**`POST /shifts/{shiftId}/reopen`**: Reopen a shift closed in error

A cashier who closed the wrong till, or a count submitted before the drawer was finished.
**A reversal, and treated as one.** Requires an approver who is not the person who closed it, and the original close is retained — reopening does not erase the first count, because the difference between the two counts is the thing worth looking at.

### setDenominations

**`PUT /denominations`**: Set the notes and coins a region's tills count

**`listDenominations` read a table nothing wrote**, so every till's count screen was empty until somebody inserted rows by hand, and the UAE seed Tanmay supplied had no way in.
**Region scope, because a currency is.** A note in circulation in the UAE is in circulation at every UAE venue; a venue that could set its own list could count a note its neighbour cannot, and the cash-ups would not add up across the region.
**Replaces the list for one currency and never deletes.** A denomination left out is deactivated, not removed, because deleting it would rewrite every past cash-up that counted it.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `REGION_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | region |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `platform.denomination` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Config scope | region |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `platform.denomination` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `platform.denomination` |
| Called by | BO-1065 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**: `SetDenominationsRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| currencyCode | string | yes | (min length 3; max length 3) |
| denominations | array of object | yes | (min items 1) |
| denominations[].displayName | string | yes | (max length 60) |
| denominations[].kind | enum (note, coin) | yes |  |
| denominations[].value | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| denominations[].value.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| denominations[].value.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| denominations[].value.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| denominations[].sortOrder | integer | yes | (min 0) |
| denominations[].isActive | boolean |  | (default True) |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | The currency's denominations, in counting order, including the ones deactivated |
| 400 | BadRequest | Validation failed |
| 403 | Forbidden | Authenticated but not permitted at the requested scope |
| 422 |  | A zero or negative value, or two entries with the same value and kind. |

### suspendShift

**`POST /shifts/{shiftId}/suspend`**: Suspend a shift so another user can log in

The break mechanism. The float and the shift stay intact; the workstation becomes available to another principal. Resuming requires the original principal, or a principal holding SHIFT_CLOSE_OTHER to close it out instead.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `SHIFT_SUSPEND` |
| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | append |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.pos_shift` |

### withdrawFromDepositBox

**`POST /deposit-boxes/{boxId}/withdraw`**: A supervisor takes cash out mid-shift

5.8.8. **A mid-day skim, and it is not a variance.** Cash removed from a till for banking must reduce the expected close figure, or every busy cashier ends the day apparently short by the amount that was taken from them.
Two people sign: the supervisor taking it and the cashier it came from.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `CASH_LIFT` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | append |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.deposit_box` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `orders.cash_movement`, `orders.deposit_box` |
| Called by | POS-013, POS-018 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| boxId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string | Client-generated ULID. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| amount | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| amount.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| amount.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |

## Group: sync

### syncOrders

**`POST /sync/orders`**: Replay orders recorded offline

Ordered batch, processed in `sequence` order, stopping at the first entry that cannot be accepted. A void must never be evaluated before the sale it voids.
Every line is re-priced on ingest. Variances are returned per order and posted to the variance account; the client surfaces nothing to the cashier.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_CREATE` |
| Scope level | workstation |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | append |
| Reads | `orders.sales_order` |
| Writes | `orders.sales_order` |
| Called by | BO-037, BO-130, BO-132, EMP-017, POS-002, POS-013, SCN-014 |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| deviceId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| orders | array of OfflineOrder | yes | (min items 1; max items 200) |
| orders[].id | string | yes | Client-generated ULID. (pattern ^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$) |
| orders[].venueId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| orders[].channel | Channel: enum (pos, kiosk, web, mobile, b2b, ota, callCentre) | yes |  |
| orders[].shiftId | string |  |  |
| orders[].subjectId | string (uuid) |  | Null for an anonymous sale. (nullable) |
| orders[].guestLinkId | string |  | Present where the guest is linked across cells. (nullable) |
| orders[].catalogueBundleVersion | string |  | The bundle the client priced from. |
| orders[].lines | array of CreateOrderLine | yes | (min items 1) |
| orders[].lines[].id | string | yes | (pattern ^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$) |
| orders[].lines[].variantId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| orders[].lines[].performanceId | string (uuid) |  |  |
| orders[].lines[].inventoryHoldId | string |  | Lease the units were drawn from. (nullable) |
| orders[].lines[].seatIds | array of string |  | Seated products only. |
| orders[].lines[].quantity | integer | yes | (min 1) |
| orders[].lines[].eligibilityDeclaration | array of object |  | What was declared for each guest on this line, kept as the record staff check at the gate. (nullable) |
| orders[].lines[].quotedUnitPrice | object | yes | What the client charged, from its local bundle. |
| orders[].lines[].holderName | string |  | (nullable) |
| orders[].lines[].dataMaskValues | object |  |  |
| orders[].recordedAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |
| orders[].sequence | integer | yes | Monotonic per device. (min 1) |
| orders[].payments | array of CreatePaymentRequest | yes |  |
| orders[].payments[].id | string | yes | (pattern ^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$) |
| orders[].payments[].orderId | string | yes |  |
| orders[].payments[].tender | TenderKind: enum (cash, card, wallet, voucher, bankTransfer, hotelCharge, installment, giftCard, …) | yes |  |
| orders[].payments[].amount | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| orders[].payments[].tenderedAmount | object |  | Cash only. |
| orders[].payments[].walletAuthorisationId | string |  | Cross-cell wallet hold, where the guest's home cell is elsewhere. (nullable) |
| orders[].payments[].deviceId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| orders[].payments[].recordedAt | string (date-time) | yes |  |

**Response**: `OrderSyncResult`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| accepted | integer | yes |  |
| stoppedAtSequence | integer |  | First entry that could not be processed. (nullable) |
| results | array of object | yes |  |
| results[].id | string | yes |  |
| results[].sequence | integer | yes |  |
| results[].status | enum (accepted, duplicate, rejected) | yes |  |
| results[].orderNumber | string |  | (nullable) |
| results[].priceVariance | object |  | Posted to the variance account. |
| results[].priceVariance.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| results[].priceVariance.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| results[].priceVariance.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| results[].varianceExceedsThreshold | boolean |  | True when review is required per the venue's variance threshold. |
| results[].error | Problem |  | RFC 9457 problem details. |
| results[].error.type | string (uri) | yes |  |
| results[].error.title | string | yes |  |
| results[].error.status | integer | yes |  |
| results[].error.detail | string |  |  |
| results[].error.instance | string |  |  |
| results[].error.traceId | string |  |  |
| results[].error.errors | array of object |  |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Batch processed, wholly or up to the first rejection |
